● All checks green. Phase 8A is complete.

  PHASE 8A RESULT

  What was built

  Piece: src/domain/notification/index.ts
  Role: Pure: the eight templates, TEMPLATE_AUDIENCE, dedupeKeyFor, the retry
  curve, the two-day expiry window, recipient parsing
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  Piece: src/lib/email/{layout,templates,transport,index}.ts
  Role: Table-based HTML + a plain-text part for every message; eight typed
  templates; one EmailTransport with Resend / memory / console implementations
  chosen by configuration, not NODE_ENV
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  Piece: src/server/repositories/notification-repository.ts
  Role: The only module touching notifications. Enqueue (swallowing the unique
  violation), skip-with-reason, lease-based claim, mark sent/failed, requeue,
  count
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  Piece: src/server/services/notification-service.ts
  Role: The eight events, dispatchNotifications, runNotificationSweep
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  Piece: src/app/api/jobs/notifications/route.ts
  Role: POST only, bearer JOBS_SECRET compared in constant time, 503
  unconfigured, counts in the response
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  Piece: prisma/migrations/20260906140000_notification_outbox/
  Role: notifications.dedupe_key + unique index; seeds
  notifications.adminRecipients empty
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  Piece: Hooks
  Role: onPlaced (order placement), onSubmitted (request), onSent (quote) — each

  fires inside the transaction it belongs to

  Architecture held: no email call in a component, route handler, repository or
  Prisma callback; Prisma stayed behind the repository boundary
  (NotificationTx/NotificationPayload re-exported under ORM-neutral names);
  domain/notification is pure.

  Findings

  Fixed

  1. Two tests failed only in the full parallel run. Not flakiness — the memory
     transport injected failures with a global counter, spent by whichever
     message dispatched first, and parallel suites enqueue into the same
     database. Replaced with failEmailsMatching(marker), matched against the
     message's own text; the two retry tests now assert on their own row's
     status and attempts. Three consecutive full runs green.
  2. The outbox claim was not exclusive. A batch updateMany incremented attempts
     but left status alone, so two dispatchers claimed the same rows (4 sends
     for 2 rows). Now a per-row conditional update with a five-minute lease —
     the same compare-and-swap the order/quote transitions use.
  3. A settings read inside a transaction deadlocked (pool is one connection
     wide). Recipients are now resolved by the caller before the transaction
     opens.
  4. renderEmail threw on a malformed payload and killed the dispatch loop.
     Returns null; the row fails without retry, the queue behind it drains.
  5. Ten of the sixteen server environment variables were never read. lib/env
     declared them and handed the parse a hand-written object listing six — so
     S3_*, STORAGE_LOCAL_ROOT and all four new e-mail variables were permanently
     undefined. Storage would have silently used the local adapter in
     production; the scheduler answered 503 with its secret correctly set, which
     is how it surfaced. The raw object is now derived from the schema's own
     keys, with tests/unit/env.test.ts asserting every declared name arrives.
  6. .env.example claimed the owner edits the recipients setting "from the 
     admin". No such screen exists; corrected.

  Deferred (recorded in tasks/CURRENT.md)

  - WhatsApp click-to-chat generator and the WhatsApp channel stub — a manual
    convenience with no delivery path; V1.5 owns templated WhatsApp.
  - In-app notification records (readAt) — nothing renders them.
  - The acceptanceValidUntil warning — the window is enforced lazily on every
    read; a second deadline template is more mail than the flow warrants.
  - Retry tuning, batching, digests — ARCHITECTURE.md puts them in V1.5.
  - An admin screen for the outbox — the operator functions exist; the UI is not
    this phase's scope.

  Open

  - Nothing schedules the sweep in this repository (the cron entry belongs to
    the deployment; .env.example carries the invocation).
  - No provider is configured — no credentials were fabricated, so the console
    transport runs and delivers nothing.
  - A warning queued during a sweep goes out on the next one (dispatch uses the
    instant the sweep began): a five-minute delay on the expiry warning only.
  - The two credential e-mails have no delivery record, by design — ADR-026.

  Constraints, verified

  Recipients are never client-supplied (customer → the row's own address; staff
  → the settings row, ADMIN_NOTIFICATION_EMAIL as bootstrap, SKIPPED row when
  neither is set). Payloads are built from the order/quote snapshots, never
  re-read from the catalogue. No internal note, storage key, /admin/ URL or
  credential reaches a customer template — asserted per template. Idempotency is
  the unique dedupe_key; three sweeps produce one warning. Delivery failure
  never rolls back the order. Tests send nothing anywhere.

  Documentation

  ARCHITECTURE.md §11 (an "As built" section), DATABASE.md (dedupe_key, the
  lease semantics of scheduledFor, the settings key, plus its own stale "no
  Prisma schema exists yet" opening), SECURITY.md (§2.1 transactional-email
  subsection, §10 payload rule), ROADMAP.md (8A done; Phase 10's deferred cron
  closed), CLAUDE.md, tasks/CURRENT.md (TASK-017), and ADR-026 — the two 
  credential e-mails bypass the outbox.

  Validation (final run)

  Format, lint, typecheck; 698 unit/component/integration tests (24 files) — run
  three times consecutively, identical; 107 Playwright tests across three
  projects; production build; architecture boundary probes; clean-database
  migrate → seed → seed again → migrate diff reports no drift → full suite green
  against it. New E2E: tests/e2e/notification.spec.ts walks request → quote →
  order → approaching expiry through the real interfaces and reads the outbox
  back, plus the scheduler endpoint's refusals.

✻ Sautéed for 57m 36s · done 11:56 AM

❯ # PHASE 13 — CUSTOMER & ADMIN COMPLETION                                      
                                                                                
  Phase 8A is PASS and fully validated.                                         
                                                                                
  The project now has:                                                          
                                                                                
  * storefront/catalogue                                                        
  * custom embroidery builder                                                   
  * media pipeline                                                              
  * cart/checkout/orders                                                        
  * quote lifecycle                                                             
  * customer portal                                                             
  * admin operations                                                            
  * payments/deposit workflow                                                   
  * transactional notification outbox/email                                     
                                                                                
  Do not modify completed architecture unnecessarily.                           
                                                                                
  ## 1. Scope first — do not guess                                              
                                                                                
  Before coding, read:                                                          
                                                                                
  * `docs/ROADMAP.md`                                                           
  * `tasks/CURRENT.md`                                                          
  * `docs/PRODUCT.md`                                                           
  * `docs/UX.md`                                                                
  * `docs/BUSINESS-RULES.md`                                                    
  * `docs/ARCHITECTURE.md`                                                      
  * `docs/DATABASE.md`                                                          
  * `docs/SECURITY.md`                                                          
                                                                                
  Then inspect the existing Phase 12 implementation.                            
                                                                                
  Phase 13 must implement **only the features explicitly assigned to Phase 13   
  by the current roadmap**.                                                     
                                                                                
  Do not automatically implement every remaining V1/V2 item.                    
                                                                                
  If the roadmap contains multiple possible Phase 13 workstreams, identify the  
  exact intended Phase 13 scope from the documentation before changing code.    
                                                                                
  ## 2. Preserve existing architecture                                          
                                                                                
  Continue using:                                                               
                                                                                
  UI                                                                            
  → server action/application service                                           
  → domain                                                                      
  → repository                                                                  
  → Prisma                                                                      
                                                                                
  Rules:                                                                        
                                                                                
  * Prisma remains behind repositories.                                         
  * Domain logic remains pure.                                                  
  * Authorization remains separate from authentication.                         
  * Better Auth admin plugin remains prohibited.                                
  * Existing state machines remain the source of truth.                         
  * Existing notification infrastructure must be reused rather than duplicated. 
  * Existing media/storage boundary must be reused.                             
  * Existing historical snapshots remain authoritative for historical records.  
                                                                                
  Do not introduce a new architectural pattern unless the existing architecture 
  genuinely cannot support the requirement.                                     
                                                                                
  ## 3. Critical audit before implementation                                    
                                                                                
  Before writing features, inspect the current code for the Phase 12 findings   
  and existing deferred items.                                                  
                                                                                
  In particular:                                                                
                                                                                
  * `depositWindowDays` remains a placeholder — do not invent its value.        
  * REFUND/returns behavior remains intentionally incomplete — do not invent a  
  returns policy.                                                               
  * guest customer directory behavior must follow the actual documented         
  business rules.                                                               
  * review moderation must not accidentally expose unverified/unauthorized      
  reviews.                                                                      
  * gallery/content/settings must not become an uncontrolled CMS.               
  * admin dark mode must use the existing design-system tokens.                 
  * admin catalogue management must preserve the published/draft/archived       
  rules.                                                                        
  * audit-log access must remain staff-only and must not expose secrets.        
                                                                                
  ## 4. Customer management                                                     
                                                                                
  If customer-management functionality is part of the documented Phase 13       
  scope, implement it using the existing authorization model.                   
                                                                                
  Requirements:                                                                 
                                                                                
  * staff can inspect customer information permitted by the business rules      
  * no credentials/session tokens/account secrets                               
  * customer data remains server-authorized                                     
  * fabricated IDs must not expose another customer's data                      
  * customer request/order/quote relationships remain scoped correctly          
  * avoid exposing unnecessary personal information                             
                                                                                
  Do not create a generic CRM.                                                  
                                                                                
  ## 5. Reviews                                                                 
                                                                                
  If review functionality is part of this phase:                                
                                                                                
  Use the existing V1 rule:                                                     
                                                                                
  > Only authenticated verified purchasers can submit reviews.                  
                                                                                
  Implement only the documented review lifecycle.                               
                                                                                
  Ensure:                                                                       
                                                                                
  * verified-purchase requirement is server-enforced                            
  * customer cannot review arbitrary products                                   
  * customer cannot manipulate rating/ownership/product identity                
  * duplicate-review rules follow the database/business rules                   
  * staff moderation is authorization-protected if moderation is in scope       
  * unapproved content is not accidentally rendered publicly                    
  * review text is escaped/safely rendered                                      
  * customer-facing DTOs do not leak moderation/internal fields                 
                                                                                
  Do not add ratings analytics or recommendation systems.                       
                                                                                
  ## 6. Gallery/content                                                         
                                                                                
  If gallery/content management is explicitly included:                         
                                                                                
  Keep it intentionally small.                                                  
                                                                                
  Use the existing media pipeline.                                              
                                                                                
  Requirements:                                                                 
                                                                                
  * private uploads remain private                                              
  * public assets use the established public asset resolver                     
  * staff-only management                                                       
  * no raw storage keys in client DTOs                                          
  * no arbitrary HTML injection                                                 
  * safe image validation                                                       
  * responsive storefront rendering                                             
  * SEO metadata where appropriate                                              
                                                                                
  Do not build a general-purpose CMS.                                           
                                                                                
  ## 7. Admin settings                                                          
                                                                                
  If settings management is in scope:                                           
                                                                                
  Reuse the existing settings infrastructure.                                   
                                                                                
  Every setting must have:                                                      
                                                                                
  * defined key                                                                 
  * explicit type                                                               
  * validation                                                                  
  * server-side authorization                                                   
  * documented purpose                                                          
  * safe fallback behavior                                                      
                                                                                
  Do not allow arbitrary key/value insertion.                                   
                                                                                
  Especially do not invent values for:                                          
                                                                                
  * `depositWindowDays`                                                         
  * shipping policy                                                             
  * pricing                                                                     
  * business rules                                                              
                                                                                
  where the owner has not defined them.                                         
                                                                                
  For values that genuinely require an owner decision, preserve the placeholder 
  and surface it clearly rather than guessing.                                  
                                                                                
  ## 8. Admin catalogue management                                              
                                                                                
  If this is part of Phase 13:                                                  
                                                                                
  Implement CRUD/workflows for the existing catalogue model without bypassing   
  Phase 6 rules.                                                                
                                                                                
  Preserve:                                                                     
                                                                                
  * draft/published/archived behavior                                           
  * soft deletion                                                               
  * product/category relationships                                              
  * variants                                                                    
  * options                                                                     
  * customization profiles                                                      
  * image ordering                                                              
  * product customization eligibility                                           
  * public visibility rules                                                     
  * server-side validation                                                      
                                                                                
  Historical orders and quotes must never change when catalogue data changes.   
                                                                                
  Do not allow catalogue edits to rewrite historical snapshots.                 
                                                                                
  ## 9. Audit log viewer                                                        
                                                                                
  If included:                                                                  
                                                                                
  Build a staff-only read interface over the existing audit events.             
                                                                                
  Requirements:                                                                 
                                                                                
  * authorization                                                               
  * bounded pagination                                                          
  * useful filters                                                              
  * newest-first ordering                                                       
  * actor/action/entity/timestamp information as permitted                      
  * no secret/token/password/storage-key exposure                               
  * no arbitrary entity lookup bypass                                           
  * no mutation capability                                                      
                                                                                
  Do not create a second audit system.                                          
                                                                                
  ## 10. Admin dark mode                                                        
                                                                                
  If explicitly part of this phase:                                             
                                                                                
  Use the existing dark-admin tokens from the design system.                    
                                                                                
  Do not introduce a second color system.                                       
                                                                                
  Verify:                                                                       
                                                                                
  * contrast                                                                    
  * focus states                                                                
  * status colors                                                               
  * tables                                                                      
  * forms                                                                       
  * dialogs                                                                     
  * empty/error states                                                          
                                                                                
  Test the major admin routes in both themes.                                   
                                                                                
  ## 11. Security                                                               
                                                                                
  For every new feature, add adversarial coverage for:                          
                                                                                
  * unauthenticated access                                                      
  * CUSTOMER accessing staff functionality                                      
  * STAFF capability boundaries                                                 
  * IDOR                                                                        
  * forged ownership                                                            
  * forged product/customer/review identifiers                                  
  * client-controlled money/status fields                                       
  * internal/admin data leakage                                                 
  * storage authorization                                                       
  * unsafe HTML/content                                                         
  * invalid enum/status transitions                                             
  * race conditions where relevant                                              
                                                                                
  Never rely only on UI hiding.                                                 
                                                                                
  ## 12. UX                                                                     
                                                                                
  Use the existing design system.                                               
                                                                                
  Maintain:                                                                     
                                                                                
  * 375 / 768 / 1024 / 1440 responsiveness                                      
  * keyboard accessibility                                                      
  * visible focus                                                               
  * semantic controls                                                           
  * loading states                                                              
  * empty states                                                                
  * error states                                                                
  * success feedback                                                            
  * confirmation for destructive operations                                     
                                                                                
  Do not create a separate admin visual language.                               
                                                                                
  ## 13. Testing                                                                
                                                                                
  Add focused:                                                                  
                                                                                
  ### Unit                                                                      
                                                                                
  Domain rules and validation.                                                  
                                                                                
  ### Integration                                                               
                                                                                
  Repositories, authorization, database constraints, state transitions and      
  security boundaries.                                                          
                                                                                
  ### E2E                                                                       
                                                                                
  Critical user/admin journeys.                                                 
                                                                                
  ### Accessibility                                                             
                                                                                
  axe checks for new/modified pages.                                            
                                                                                
  ### Architecture                                                              
                                                                                
  Existing probes must continue to pass.                                        
                                                                                
  Then run:                                                                     
                                                                                
  * lint                                                                        
  * typecheck                                                                   
  * full unit/integration suite                                                 
  * full Playwright suite                                                       
  * production build                                                            
  * fresh database migration                                                    
  * seed                                                                        
  * second seed                                                                 
  * migration diff                                                              
  * fresh-DB integration tests                                                  
  * repeated critical E2E flows                                                 
                                                                                
  Do not weaken existing tests.                                                 
                                                                                
  ## 14. Regression protection                                                  
                                                                                
  Phases 7–12 are already validated.                                            
                                                                                
  Do not regress:                                                               
                                                                                
  * custom builder                                                              
  * uploads                                                                     
  * cart                                                                        
  * checkout                                                                    
  * stock reservation                                                           
  * quote acceptance                                                            
  * quote-bound carts                                                           
  * customer portal                                                             
  * admin orders                                                                
  * payments/deposits                                                           
  * notifications                                                               
                                                                                
  If an existing test fails, determine whether the new feature caused a         
  regression before changing the test.                                          
                                                                                
  ## 15. Documentation                                                          
                                                                                
  Update only documentation affected by the implementation:                     
                                                                                
  * `docs/ROADMAP.md`                                                           
  * `tasks/CURRENT.md`                                                          
  * relevant architecture/database/security/UX documentation                    
  * `CLAUDE.md` only if project guidance has materially changed                 
  * ADR only if a genuine new architectural decision was introduced             
                                                                                
  Explicitly record anything still blocked on an owner/business decision.       
                                                                                
  ## 16. Strict exclusions                                                      
                                                                                
  Do NOT implement unrelated:                                                   
                                                                                
  * V2 analytics                                                                
  * marketing                                                                   
  * abandoned cart                                                              
  * SMS/WhatsApp                                                                
  * online payment providers                                                    
  * carrier APIs                                                                
  * production scheduling                                                       
  * coupons                                                                     
  * wishlist                                                                    
  * reorder                                                                     
  * recommendation engine                                                       
  * arbitrary CMS functionality                                                 
  * returns/refund policy not yet defined                                       
  * guessed business settings                                                   
                                                                                
  ## 17. Final response                                                         
                                                                                
  Return exactly:                                                               
                                                                                
  ## PHASE 13 RESULT                                                            
                                                                                
  ### Scope implemented                                                         
                                                                                
  ### Customer                                                                  
                                                                                
  ### Reviews                                                                   
                                                                                
  ### Gallery / content                                                         
                                                                                
  ### Settings                                                                  
                                                                                
  ### Catalogue                                                                 
                                                                                
  ### Audit log                                                                 
                                                                                
  ### Admin dark mode                                                           
                                                                                
  ### Security                                                                  
                                                                                
  ### UX                                                                        
                                                                                
  ### Tests                                                                     
                                                                                
  ### Documentation                                                             
                                                                                
  ### Findings                                                                  
                                                                                
  ### Phase 13 status                                                           
                                                                                
  ### Next phase readiness                                                      
                                                                                
  Only include sections that actually apply to the documented Phase 13 scope.   
                                                                                
  For every finding label it:                                                   
                                                                                
  * fixed                                                                       
  * open                                                                        
  * deferred                                                                    
                                                                                
  Do not start another phase in the same implementation pass.                   
                                                                                
  If the roadmap's Phase 13 scope is ambiguous, resolve it from the existing    
  documentation and implementation rather than inventing functionality.

● PHASE 13 RESULT

  Scope implemented

  ROADMAP.md's Phase 13 entry — Reviews, Gallery, Content Polish — and only
  that. Its five bullets, all delivered:

  - review submission restricted to delivered purchases
  - review display on product pages with the rating distribution
  - gallery filtering, detail pages, links to related products
  - trust elements: why-us, delivery information, quality guarantee, social
    section
  - homepage section types all implemented and reorderable

  Deliberately excluded, resolved from the documentation rather than assumed:
  customer management, settings screens, admin catalogue management, the
  audit-log viewer and admin dark mode are listed in ROADMAP.md under Phase 12's
  outstanding half, not Phase 13. Gallery curation and content management are
  in that same list — Phase 13 builds the storefront that reads those rows; the
  screens that edit them are not this phase. Review moderation is the exception:
  Phase 13's "done when" requires that moderation control visibility, so it is
  built here.

  New layers: domain/{review,gallery,content},
  server/repositories/{review,gallery,content}-repository,
  server/services/{review,content}-service, routes /gallery, /gallery/[slug],
  /account/reviews, /admin/reviews, plus the product page's review section and a
  homepage composed from homepage_sections. One migration (gallery consent
  CHECK, published-gallery index, moderation-queue index). Prisma stayed behind
  the repositories; the domain stayed pure; all four boundary probes pass.

  Reviews

  11A implemented end to end. A review's payload names an order line and nothing
  else — the product comes from the line, the line is resolved with
  order.userId in the query, isVerifiedPurchase is written from the resolved
  delivered order, and the status is always PENDING. There is no path through
  the service that publishes anything.

  Moderation approves or rejects and recomputes the rating from the approved 
  rows inside the same transaction (11A.3) — recomputed, never incremented,
  because an incremental aggregate drifts with nothing to notice. The update is
  conditional on the status the moderator was looking at, so two moderators
  cannot both believe they decided. Approving revalidates the product's path, so
  it appears without waiting out the five-minute ISR window.

  products.ratingAverage is the mean × 100 and is null with no reviews, not 
  zero.

  Gallery / content

  /gallery filters by link, so a filtered view is a shareable URL that survives
  the back button and needs no client component. Detail pages join related
  products on shared tags. Published-and-not-deleted is a clause in every query.

  The homepage is homepage_sections rows (ADR-027): eleven band types, ordered
  by position, switched by isEnabled. domain/content declares what each type
  needs and content-service loads the union once — a switched-off band issues no
  query. A band with nothing to show renders nothing: the reviews band is
  approved rows or absent, and the trust band's delivery figures come from
  shipping_rates rather than typed-in copy, so a front-page promise cannot drift
  from what the checkout charges.

  CATEGORY_GRID is implemented and tested but seeded disabled: its cards lead to
  /shop/c/[category], which Phase 6 has not built.

  Security

  Only APPROVED reviews are public, in the where. The public DTO carries a
  shortened display name and a date — a test serialises the whole panel and
  asserts no e-mail, user id, moderatedByUserId or moderatedAt appears. Review
  text is rendered as text; nothing on that path sets inner HTML. A foreign or
  invented order-line id returns NOT_FOUND, indistinguishable from a nonexistent
  one. Every admin action calls requireStaff and then the capability predicate;
  a customer typing /admin/reviews is redirected home. No gallery DTO carries
  sourceDesignFileId, imageKey or customerConsentAt. Content config gets
  defaults for anything malformed, bounded limits, and icons through a closed
  allow-list.

  13.6 is now enforced twice — the domain predicate and a CHECK constraint —
  because it is the one mistake here that ends a business rather than filing a
  bug.

  UX

  Design-system tokens only, no new colour. The rating control is a real radio
  group (arrow keys, announced, submitted by the browser); the stars are
  aria-hidden and the meaning is in text. axe reports no WCAG 2.1 A/AA
  violations on /, /gallery, /gallery/[slug], /account/reviews, /admin/reviews
  or the product page with reviews, and none of them overflows at 375px.
  Loading/empty/error/success states on every new screen.

  Tests

  783 unit, component and integration tests (28 files, +85) and 117 Playwright
  tests (+10). New: tests/unit/{review,content}-domain.test.ts,
  tests/integration/{review,content}.test.ts,
  tests/e2e/{review,content}.spec.ts. The review E2E buys, takes delivery,
  writes, checks the review is not on the product page, publishes it from the
  admin, and checks it is — with the recomputed rating.

  Documentation

  ROADMAP.md (Phase 13 done; Phase 12's list corrected), tasks/CURRENT.md
  (TASK-018), BUSINESS-RULES.md (11A.5–11A.7, three rows in the centralisation
  table), DATABASE.md (the constraint, two indexes, homepage_sections in use),
  SECURITY.md (§10), ARCHITECTURE.md (§11, "the homepage is data"), UX.md
  (sitemap), CLAUDE.md, and ADR-027 — the homepage is rows, not a component.

  Findings

  - fixed — aria-pressed on the gallery filter chips: not permitted on a link's
    role. Now aria-current. Found by axe in the new E2E spec, not by review.
  - fixed — the admin reply box had no label: the component overrode the id that
    FormField generates. Also caught by axe.
  - fixed — the star radios were sr-only beside their labels, so the control had
    no pointer target. The input now covers the 44px box.
  - fixed — a stale allow-list in tests/components/storefront.test.tsx (nav
    links vs. routes that exist) failed when /gallery landed. It is now derived
    from the route files, so it cannot go stale again.
  - open — no admin screens for gallery curation, content or settings. Editing
    those rows is a database update until Phase 12's outstanding half is built.
    This is the largest thing the phase leaves open.
  - open — /shop/c/[category] does not exist, so CATEGORY_GRID ships disabled.
  - open — /faq, /about, /how-it-works, /size-guide, /care-guide, /privacy,
    /terms, /contact are in UX.md's sitemap, backed by the pages and faqs
    tables, and claimed by no phase. Somebody should assign them.
  - open — no photography: gallery keys point at paths the public bucket does
    not have yet, so the placeholders render.
  - deferred — editing or withdrawing one's own review (11A describes neither;
    the policy is a business decision).
  - deferred — the "create an account to review this order" prompt for guest
    purchasers (11A.4's documented mitigation).
  - deferred — gallery pagination (bounded at 24, like the catalogue).
  - untouched, as instructed — depositWindowDays remains a placeholder; refunds
    still change no status.

  Phase 13 status

  PASS. Format, lint, typecheck, 783 tests, 117 Playwright tests, the production
  build, the architecture probes, and a clean-database migrate → seed → seed
  again → migrate diff (no drift) → full suite all pass. Critical E2E flows
  re-run afterwards with the same result. No existing test was weakened; the one
  that changed was strengthened.

  Next phase readiness

  ROADMAP.md next names Phase 14 — SEO, which now has both surfaces it was
  waiting on: the gallery has canonical URLs and Open Graph metadata, and
  products carry an aggregate rating for Product structured data. The larger
  outstanding block is Phase 12's admin half; ADR-027 makes the content screens
  a straightforward CRUD over rows the storefront already reads. Nothing from
  Phase 14 or beyond was started.

✻ Worked for 57m 20s · done 3:50 PM

  * `docs/PRODUCT.md`                                                           
  * `docs/ARCHITECTURE.md`                                                      
  * `docs/SECURITY.md`                                                          
                                                                                
  Then inspect the existing:                                                    
                                                                                
  * Next.js metadata implementation                                             
  * product pages                                                               
  * gallery pages                                                               
  * homepage                                                                    
  * sitemap/robots implementation, if present                                   
  * review/rating implementation from Phase 13                                  
  * image/asset handling                                                        
  * public route structure                                                      
                                                                                
  Do not reread the entire repository.                                          
                                                                                
  ## 2. Scope                                                                   
                                                                                
  Implement only the SEO requirements assigned to Phase 14.                     
                                                                                
  Prioritize:                                                                   
                                                                                
  * metadata                                                                    
  * canonical URLs                                                              
  * Open Graph / social metadata                                                
  * sitemap                                                                     
  * robots                                                                      
  * structured data                                                             
  * product structured data using the approved rating aggregate                 
  * gallery/page discoverability                                                
  * appropriate indexing controls                                               
  * SEO-safe handling of dynamic/public routes                                  
                                                                                
  Do not invent SEO features outside the documented phase.                      
                                                                                
  ## 3. Metadata                                                                
                                                                                
  Ensure important public pages have appropriate server-generated metadata.     
                                                                                
  At minimum inspect:                                                           
                                                                                
  * homepage                                                                    
  * shop                                                                        
  * category pages if currently public                                          
  * product detail pages                                                        
  * custom embroidery page                                                      
  * gallery listing                                                             
  * gallery detail                                                              
  * relevant public content/trust pages that actually exist                     
                                                                                
  Metadata must be derived from authoritative server-side data.                 
                                                                                
  Do not expose:                                                                
                                                                                
  * draft content                                                               
  * unpublished products                                                        
  * deleted gallery entries                                                     
  * private customer information                                                
  * internal/admin routes                                                       
  * private design files                                                        
                                                                                
  Dynamic metadata must not accidentally turn a 404 resource into an indexable  
  page.                                                                         
                                                                                
  ## 4. Canonical URLs                                                          
                                                                                
  Implement canonical URLs for public indexable pages.                          
                                                                                
  Rules:                                                                        
                                                                                
  * canonical URLs must be absolute and use the configured canonical site       
  origin                                                                        
  * query/filter parameters must not create unintended duplicate canonical      
  pages                                                                         
  * gallery filters should behave intentionally according to the SEO            
  requirements                                                                  
  * product canonical URLs should use the product's public slug                 
  * deleted/draft/unpublished resources must not receive public canonical       
  metadata                                                                      
  * quote/account/admin/custom-request routes must not become indexable         
  accidentally                                                                  
                                                                                
  Do not hard-code a production domain if the project already has an            
  environment/configuration source for the site origin.                         
                                                                                
  ## 5. Open Graph / social metadata                                            
                                                                                
  Add appropriate Open Graph metadata for public pages where required.          
                                                                                
  Use:                                                                          
                                                                                
  * page title                                                                  
  * description                                                                 
  * canonical URL                                                               
  * appropriate public image where available                                    
                                                                                
  Do not expose private storage URLs.                                           
                                                                                
  Use the existing public asset/image resolver.                                 
                                                                                
  If a public image is unavailable, use the documented fallback rather than     
  inventing a broken asset URL.                                                 
                                                                                
  ## 6. Sitemap                                                                 
                                                                                
  Implement or verify a production sitemap containing only appropriate          
  public/indexable URLs.                                                        
                                                                                
  Include relevant:                                                             
                                                                                
  * homepage                                                                    
  * shop                                                                        
  * public categories                                                           
  * published products                                                          
  * gallery pages that should be indexed                                        
  * other public SEO pages explicitly included by UX/ROADMAP                    
                                                                                
  Do not include:                                                               
                                                                                
  * admin                                                                       
  * account                                                                     
  * checkout/cart                                                               
  * quote/request pages                                                         
  * private media                                                               
  * unpublished/draft/deleted records                                           
  * arbitrary query/filter combinations                                         
                                                                                
  Dynamic URLs must be generated from authoritative repositories/services.      
                                                                                
  Keep the sitemap bounded and deterministic.                                   
                                                                                
  ## 7. Robots                                                                  
                                                                                
  Implement or verify `robots.txt`.                                             
                                                                                
  It must prevent crawling/indexing of private/application surfaces where       
  appropriate, especially:                                                      
                                                                                
  * `/admin`                                                                    
  * `/account`                                                                  
  * `/cart`                                                                     
  * `/checkout`                                                                 
  * private/custom workflow routes where applicable                             
                                                                                
  Do not block public storefront/product/gallery pages.                         
                                                                                
  Do not use robots.txt as a substitute for authorization.                      
                                                                                
  ## 8. Structured data                                                         
                                                                                
  Implement structured data only where justified by the actual content.         
                                                                                
  For product pages, use the existing product information and Phase 13 rating   
  aggregate.                                                                    
                                                                                
  Where applicable include:                                                     
                                                                                
  * Product                                                                     
  * Offer                                                                       
  * AggregateRating                                                             
                                                                                
  Only emit `AggregateRating` when there are approved reviews and a valid       
  aggregate.                                                                    
                                                                                
  Never fabricate:                                                              
                                                                                
  * review counts                                                               
  * ratings                                                                     
  * prices                                                                      
  * availability                                                                
  * shipping claims                                                             
                                                                                
  Structured data must use the same authoritative values presented to users.    
                                                                                
  If a product has no approved reviews, omit `AggregateRating` rather than      
  emitting empty/zero fake data.                                                
                                                                                
  Validate structured-data output for:                                          
                                                                                
  * published products                                                          
  * products with reviews                                                       
  * products without reviews                                                    
  * unavailable/draft products                                                  
                                                                                
  ## 9. SEO data integrity                                                      
                                                                                
  SEO metadata must not become a second business-data source.                   
                                                                                
  For example:                                                                  
                                                                                
  * product price comes from the authoritative product/variant representation   
  * rating comes from the approved review aggregate                             
  * availability follows the actual public product state                        
  * shipping claims come from authoritative shipping data if surfaced           
  * gallery publication follows gallery visibility rules                        
                                                                                
  Do not duplicate mutable business rules merely for SEO.                       
                                                                                
  ## 10. Security                                                               
                                                                                
  Add tests ensuring:                                                           
                                                                                
  * unpublished products do not appear in sitemap                               
  * deleted gallery items do not appear in sitemap                              
  * private/account/admin routes are not exposed as public URLs                 
  * draft content is not included                                               
  * private media/storage keys never enter metadata                             
  * customer information never enters public metadata                           
  * fabricated dynamic paths produce proper not-found behavior                  
  * structured data cannot be manipulated through client payloads               
                                                                                
  ## 11. Accessibility / UX                                                     
                                                                                
  SEO changes must not alter visible content incorrectly.                       
                                                                                
  If visible SEO content changes:                                               
                                                                                
  * preserve semantic headings                                                  
  * preserve accessibility                                                      
  * preserve responsive behavior                                                
  * run axe on affected routes                                                  
                                                                                
  Do not stuff keywords or create unnatural copy.                               
                                                                                
  SEO should improve discoverability without degrading the premium storefront   
  experience.                                                                   
                                                                                
  ## 12. Testing                                                                
                                                                                
  Add focused tests for:                                                        
                                                                                
  ### Unit                                                                      
                                                                                
  * metadata generation                                                         
  * canonical URL generation                                                    
  * structured-data generation                                                  
  * sitemap filtering                                                           
  * robots rules                                                                
                                                                                
  ### Integration                                                               
                                                                                
  * published vs unpublished/deleted resources                                  
  * rating aggregate handling                                                   
  * authoritative price/availability values                                     
                                                                                
  ### E2E                                                                       
                                                                                
  Verify representative public pages expose the expected metadata/structured    
  data and private routes remain appropriately excluded.                        
                                                                                
  Run:                                                                          
                                                                                
  * lint                                                                        
  * typecheck                                                                   
  * all unit/integration tests                                                  
  * all Playwright tests                                                        
  * accessibility tests                                                         
  * architecture probes                                                         
  * production build                                                            
  * fresh database migration                                                    
  * seed                                                                        
  * second seed                                                                 
  * migrate diff                                                                
  * fresh DB integration suite                                                  
  * repeat critical E2E flows                                                   
                                                                                
  Do not weaken existing tests.                                                 
                                                                                
  ## 13. Documentation                                                          
                                                                                
  Update only relevant documentation:                                           
                                                                                
  * `docs/ROADMAP.md`                                                           
  * `tasks/CURRENT.md`                                                          
  * `docs/UX.md` if sitemap/public-route behavior changes                       
  * `docs/ARCHITECTURE.md` if SEO architecture needs recording                  
  * `docs/SECURITY.md` if public metadata/security boundaries change            
  * environment documentation if a site-origin variable is required             
                                                                                
  Add an ADR only if a genuine architectural decision is introduced.            
                                                                                
  Also keep the previously noted unassigned public routes visible in            
  `tasks/CURRENT.md`; do not silently invent their ownership or content.        
                                                                                
  ## 14. Explicit exclusions                                                    
                                                                                
  Do NOT implement:                                                             
                                                                                
  * Phase 15                                                                    
  * analytics                                                                   
  * Google Analytics                                                            
  * marketing automation                                                        
  * SEO dashboards                                                              
  * backlink tooling                                                            
  * keyword tracking                                                            
  * paid SEO tools                                                              
  * content strategy beyond what the roadmap requires                           
  * new CMS features                                                            
  * customer management                                                         
  * admin catalogue management                                                  
  * audit-log UI                                                                
  * refund/returns logic                                                        
  * deposit-window business decisions                                           
  * unrelated V2 features
