● All green. Phase 14 is complete.

  PHASE 14 RESULT

  Scope implemented

  ROADMAP.md's Phase 14 — SEO — and only that. New: domain/seo (pure JSON-LD
  builders), lib/seo (the single place a public URL, canonical or robots
  directive is produced), components/seo (the JSON-LD element and the drawn
  social card), app/sitemap.ts, app/robots.ts, two Open Graph image routes,
  product_redirects read at last, and one migration fixing a defect this phase's
  test runs exposed. Nothing from Phase 15 was started; no analytics, Search
  Console, dashboards or keyword tooling.

  Three roadmap bullets could not be delivered as written and are recorded as
  open rather than faked: 410 for archived products, FAQPage (no /faq route
  exists), category URLs (no /shop/c/[category] route exists).

  Metadata

  One helper, publicPageMetadata, builds title, description, canonical, Open
  Graph and Twitter from the same three values, so a page cannot end up with a
  canonical that disagrees with its og:url. Applied to /, /shop, /shop/[slug],
  /custom, /gallery, /gallery/[slug]. Product metadata prefers the
  seoTitle/seoDescription columns and falls back to the customer-facing name and
  description. generateMetadata that cannot resolve its row returns
  notFoundMetadata — a plain title, noindex, no canonical.

  Canonicals

  Absolute, always from NEXT_PUBLIC_SITE_URL; no domain is written anywhere.
  absoluteUrl drops the query string and normalises the trailing slash, so one
  page has one address. Filtered gallery views canonicalise to /gallery and
  carry noindex, follow. Private routes carry noindex and no canonical.

  Open Graph

  Every public page has a card. Products get a generated one carrying the name
  and price; the whole site has a brand-only fallback. A product with a real
  photograph keeps the photograph — Next uses the file-based image only when the
  page's metadata sets none. No storage key or signed URL ever reaches
  metadata; images resolve through the existing public asset resolver, and an
  unavailable one is omitted rather than invented.

  Sitemap

  Generated from the repositories behind the storefront's own visibility
  predicates — publiclyVisibleWhere, publiclyVisibleGalleryWhere — not a second
  definition of "public". Four static routes plus published products and gallery
  items, bounded at 10 000 each, with lastModified from the row. No admin,
  account, cart, checkout, order, quote, request or API path; no query strings;
  no duplicates. Empty while the deployment is not indexable.

  Robots

  A written-out disallow list: /admin, /account, /cart, /checkout, /login,
  /register, /order, /dev, /api; /shop, /gallery and /custom untouched. Points
  at the sitemap. Disallows everything while the deployment is not indexable.
  Documented — in the file and in SECURITY.md — as not an access control.

  Structured data

  Organization + WebSite on the homepage; BreadcrumbList on both listings and
  both detail types; ItemList on /shop and /gallery; Product + Offer on product
  pages, with AggregateRating only when there is at least one approved review
  and a computed average. Price, availability, category and SKU are the values
  the page renders — the price formatted with the same fraction digits
  formatMoney uses, so the two agree by construction. Verified on: a published
  product with no reviews (no rating node), one with reviews, an out-of-stock
  one, and a draft (404, nothing emitted).

  Security

  Public metadata is built from public DTOs only; a test serialises the product
  panel and asserts no e-mail, user id or moderation field appears.
  sourceDesignFileId, imageKey and customerConsentAt stay out of every gallery
  DTO and therefore out of every <head>. Draft products and unpublished or
  deleted gallery items are absent from the sitemap and unreachable, with no
  canonical. JSON-LD is escaped so a product name containing </script> cannot
  close the element, and nothing a request supplies reaches a builder.

  UX / accessibility

  One visible change: brand.tagline and brand.description are now French — they
  are the site <title>, the meta description and the line under the logo on a
  lang="fr" site. No headings, structure or responsive behaviour changed; the
  full axe coverage was re-run.

  Tests

  819 unit/component/integration (+36) and 132 Playwright (+15). New:
  tests/unit/seo-domain.test.ts, tests/unit/seo-metadata.test.ts (both indexing
  states, by re-importing the module), tests/integration/seo.test.ts (sitemap
  and robots against the real database, both states), tests/e2e/seo.spec.ts (the
  served robots.txt and sitemap.xml, the rendered <head>, parsed JSON-LD, the
  generated card, the 301 redirect, 404s that claim nothing, private routes
  carrying noindex).

  Documentation

  ROADMAP.md (Phase 14 done; Phase 18 gains the switch and the Lighthouse
  check), tasks/CURRENT.md (TASK-019, with the unowned routes repeated
  verbatim), ARCHITECTURE.md (new §11A), SECURITY.md (§10), UX.md (the
  machine-readable routes), DECISIONS.md (ADR-023 amended; ADR-028 — indexing is
  a deployment-wide switch, off by default), .env.example.

  Findings

  - fixed — Human identifiers wrapped at ten thousand. lpad(n::text, 4, '0')
    truncates in PostgreSQL, so the 10 000th request became DEM-2026-1046 and
    collided with an issued number — a unique-constraint violation at the moment
    a customer submits. Latent since Phase 8; surfaced because this phase's
    repeated E2E runs pushed the sequence past 9999. Fixed by migration;
    to_char(n,'FM0000') was tried first and is wrong (it renders #### on
    overflow). ADR-023 updated; E2E regexes that read a number off the screen
    were matching \d{4} and would have captured four digits of five.
  - fixed — the site <title> and meta description were English on a French site.
  - fixed — /cart had a canonical and no robots directive.
  - fixed — a global alternates.canonical I briefly added to the root layout
    gave the 404 page and the cart a canonical pointing at the homepage; the E2E
    caught it.
  - open — 410 for archived products. The App Router cannot set a status from a
    page; it needs middleware or a route handler, and Prisma does not run at the
    edge. Archived products return 404 today.
  - open — FAQPage, and the eight storefront routes (/faq, /about,
    /how-it-works, /size-guide, /care-guide, /privacy, /terms, /contact) that
    UX.md lists, the pages/faqs tables back, and no phase owns. Repeated from
    Phase 13; still unassigned, and not invented here.
  - open — no category URLs, because /shop/c/[category] does not exist (Phase
    6's unfinished half).
  - open — product_redirects is read but never written; the write belongs to
    admin catalogue management.
  - open — ADR-012 calls the money column "minor units" while the application 
    treats it as whole dinars. Display, cart and structured data now agree, so
    nothing is wrong today, but the ADR and the code use the same words
    differently and that must be resolved before a currency with subunits is
    added.
  - open — no Lighthouse score. Lighthouse cannot score a deployment that asks
    not to be indexed; the roadmap now makes it a Phase 18 launch check.
  - deferred — real Open Graph photography (no public asset bucket); sitemap
    generation at build time (the project keeps the database out of next build).

  Phase 14 status

  PASS. Format, lint, typecheck, 819 tests, 132 Playwright tests, the production
  build, four architecture probes, and a clean-database migrate → seed → seed
  again → migrate diff (no drift) → full suite all pass; critical E2E flows
  re-run afterwards with the same result. No existing test was weakened — the
  two that changed were widened to accept the identifier format the fix
  restores.

  Next phase readiness

  ROADMAP.md next names Phase 15 — Security Hardening, which is untouched; its
  first item, login throttling, is already the standing "weakest item" note in
  SECURITY.md §2.1. Phase 18 now carries two new obligations: set
  SEO_INDEXABLE=true, and take the Lighthouse measurement once it is on. Nothing
  from Phase 15 was started.
